1105 — suspension twist

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1105 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: plank.

From a plank with feet in straps, you twist the hips side to side. It trains the obliques.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Start in a plank with your feet in the straps.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1105.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Place open palms about shoulder-width apart below the shoulders, fingers forward and wrists comfortably aligned; source-defined elbow motion remains authoritative.

Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1105.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Start in a plank with your feet in the straps. 1105.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keep your arms straight. 1105.json#/instructions/1

  4. Return to centre. 1105.json#/instructions/3
  5. Rotate to the other side. 1105.json#/instructions/4
  6. Keep your shoulders steady. 1105.json#/instructions/5

11051 — cable standing high cross neutral grip triceps extension

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 11051 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A triceps pushdown with the cable set high and the handles crossed, using a neutral grip. It isolates the back of the arm.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Set both pulleys high and stand in the middle, holding the handles with palms facing in. Cross your hands in front of your chest with elbows tucked to your sides.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 11051.json#/instructions/0, 11051.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Set both pulleys high and stand in the middle, holding the handles with palms facing in. Cross your hands in front of your chest with elbows tucked to your sides.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 11051.json#/instructions/0, 11051.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Use a comfortable hip-width stance with soft knees unless the source specifies a single-leg, split or wider stance; keep the documented support foot grounded.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 11051.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Adjustable cable station. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 11051.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Set both pulleys high and stand in the middle, holding the handles with palms facing in. 11051.json#/instructions/0
  2. Cross your hands in front of your chest with elbows tucked to your sides. 11051.json#/instructions/1
  3. Press the handles down and out until your arms are straight. 11051.json#/instructions/2
  4. Squeeze your triceps for a moment. 11051.json#/instructions/3
  5. Let the handles rise back slowly without moving your elbows. 11051.json#/instructions/4

11053 — smith staggered stance hip thrust

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 11053 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

A hip thrust on the Smith machine with a staggered stance. It builds the glutes.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit with your upper back on a bench and the bar over your hips.

Arms Hands

Rest the arms beside the torso with relaxed hands on the documented support surface; this is a balance/staging choice, not an added pressing action.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit with your upper back on a bench and the bar over your hips. 11053.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stagger your feet, one slightly ahead. 11053.json#/instructions/1

  4. Lift your hips to full extension. 11053.json#/instructions/3
  5. Squeeze your glutes. 11053.json#/instructions/4
  6. Lower slowly and switch the stance. 11053.json#/instructions/5

11055 — lever belt sumo deadlift (v bar)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 11055 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A sumo deadlift on a belt squat style lever machine holding a V bar. It loads the legs and hips with less strain on the back.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand wide on the platform with toes angled out and grip the V bar. Sit your hips back, chest up, and keep your arms straight.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 11055.json#/instructions/0, 11055.json#/instructions/1

  5. Lower under control, letting your knees track over your toes. 11055.json#/instructions/4

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 11056 after decompression) · Source record

A seated machine move where you pull your shoulder blades together. It strengthens the muscles between your shoulder blades.
